# INVENTORY — Mathematics in @539Labs prompt posts to @grok (Category A) — 2026-11-10
REPORTS ONLY. Catalog / inventory, not a proof audit, not a math-grade filing. **No CERT. Nothing adopted.** Not a tip edit, not a Written premise, not a PP grade. Every item below is a **CLAIM quoted from a public X post**; nothing is proved, refuted, checked, or continued here.
Owner direction: typed chat t4476u, 2026-11-10 ~1:31 PM CST (America/Chicago) — "The prompts to the Grok replies contain the math." Working interpretation (Physicist's, not the Owner's wording): extract and catalog the mathematics in Arvin's own posts that served as prompts to @grok, not primarily Grok's replies.
Author account: @539Labs, X user id 1961794934307999744 (Arvin Hampton). All times America/Chicago, labeled CST (UTC−6) or CDT (UTC−5) as in force on the date. Generated 2026-11-10 (America/Chicago).
Zero X API calls were made for this catalog (no shared read budget used). No browser, no user-X plugin, no sign-in, nothing posted/liked/followed/bookmarked/DMed. No media opened.

## 2. Method
Scratch dir `/workspace/xinv-prompt-math/` (new; nothing in project folders). Scripts, in run order: `load.py` (loads cache + parses inventory Appendix A, CST/CDT conversion), `classify.py` (math-signal features), `topics.py` (topic regexes, period, truncation), `build.py` (per-post records → `recs.json`), `recur.py` (recurrence regexes → `recur.json`), `gen.py` (this report). All are deterministic regex/heuristic passes over text; no model judgment is encoded in counts. Curated cluster quotes in Section 6 were chosen by reading the machine-sorted listings `p3_core.txt` and `p1_top.txt`.

**Text normalization for quoting.** Leading @-mention runs are stripped; HTML entities from the API (`&lt;` `&gt;` `&amp;`) are unescaped; newlines are shown as ` ⏎ `; `|` is escaped for Markdown. Otherwise the text is verbatim as returned by the API, including the API's duplicated rendering of copied LaTeX (e.g. `T539.9=d+…   T_{539.9} = d + …   T539.9​=…`, which is how the post text itself reads). No formula has been completed or re-typeset.

**Math classifier (per post, on body without t.co links).** Feature counts: `latex` (\\[, \\(, \\frac, \\text, ^{ _{ …), `uni` (Unicode math/Greek/super- and subscript characters), `eq` (an `=`, `≈`, `≡`, `≤`, `≥`, `<`, `>`, `∝`, `~`, `mod` between symbol-like tokens), `unit` (numbers with physical units, decimals, ×10 / 10^ exponents), `kw` (math/physics keywords: theorem, lemma, conjecture, equation, derivation, proof, eigen*, modular, Hecke, p-adic, adelic, congruence, lattice, tensor, metric, Lagrangian, curvature, torsion, manifold, residual, algebra, constant, Lean, …).
- **Tier F — explicit formula / numerical claim**: any LaTeX; or ≥1 relation with any other signal; or ≥2 relations; or ≥2 math characters with a unit/keyword; or ≥2 unit-numbers.
- **Tier N — named mathematical statement without explicit formula** (theorem/framework/status claims, single constants): ≥2 keywords, or a keyword plus a digit/math character/unit, or a named framework term (Bridge C, Resonant/Residual Ternary Algebra, Einstein–Hampton, 17 Theorems, 9 Maths, Theorem Lemma, HQCC/HGCC, Collatz, T₃, 539, 11DM) with a keyword.
- **Non-math**: everything else (requests such as "Explore and discuss", "Integrate and derive", legal/political questions, thanks, ritual openings, link-only or media-only posts).
- Spot check (two random samples of 30 each, read by hand): non-math sample contained directives that *name* a math task without stating any math (e.g. "Derive the effects of the negPBH and flux on the rise of the quark-gluon strong force", 2022576447395659957) — treated as non-math by design; Tier N sample was mostly framework/status claims, with a few low-content hits (e.g. "Fetched the equation for you."). Treat F/N boundaries as ±5–10 % approximate.

**Claim fragments.** Each math post is split at sentence ends / newlines / semicolons; a fragment is kept if it carries any math signal. Fragments are normalized (lower-case, whitespace-collapsed) and de-duplicated across the corpus → *distinct extracted claim fragments*. This is a mechanical upper-bound proxy for "distinct claims" (one long derivation can yield several fragments; cross-posted copies collapse).

**Truncation flag.** The API caps long-form text at ~280 characters. Body length (mentions stripped, each t.co link counted as 23) shows a sharp pile-up at 265–284 characters (≈1,350 posts) and almost nothing above; posts with weighted body length ≥ 265 are flagged **TRUNCATED (likely)**. A post exactly at the limit could be complete; a long post can never be recovered from this cache.

**Pasted-voice flag.** 94 math posts read as pasted assistant output or third-person framing (open with a quotation mark, "Public Grok — …", "Arvin, I executed…", "Arvin Hampton's …"). They are still his posts (Category A) and are catalogued as his prompt text, but the wording may originate from another tool (e.g. his personal Grok sessions). Flag only; not verified.

## 3. Prompt definition and Grok-answered counts
- Prompt corpus = all 5,603 Category A posts (his posts tagging or replying to @grok).
- **Replies to a Grok post** (his post's parent is a Grok post): strong = 2,321 (API `in_reply_to_user_id` = Grok id 1720665183188922368, or the parent was captured with Grok as author); weak = 1,567 (parent captured without author, parent text opens with an @Brad75138648402 / @539Labs mention, and the parent is not one of his own posts — almost always Grok, not guaranteed). The inventory's "2,388 direct replies" figure counts the in_reply_to field differently; this catalog uses its own definition above.
- **Grok-answered prompts** (a captured Grok post or captured parent-of-his-reply has this post as its replied-to parent): 3,231. This is a floor: Grok replies he did not answer, and pre-rename Grok replies, were only sampled by the parent pass.
- Union (replies-to-Grok or Grok-answered): 4,456 of 5,603.
- Uncertainty: the API dropped `in_reply_to_user_id`/`conversation_id` on about half the pages, so the strong/weak split is unclean; every catalog row carries its own flag.

## 7. Grok replies — context only (not the backbone; not proofs)
Grok's reply text was used only to locate prompts (e.g. the echo-delay prompt above). The parent inventory §4 lists Grok-side remarks (arithmetic confirmations, criticisms, L5-only Lean status, peer-review caveats). None of them is treated here as confirming or refuting any catalogued claim.

## 8. Gaps and limitations
- **Truncation**: 1,079 of 1,918 math prompts are likely cut at ~280 characters; many long derivations end mid-formula (e.g. 2111682020828963080 ends "E_d ≤ (1−0.2272)O_d for"). No note_post bodies were available in the cache, and the parent pass found the long-form field returned nothing extra. No X reads were made to recover text, so the shared budget was not touched; truncated formulas are left incomplete.
- **Media**: 564 prompt posts carry media (not opened). Only 120 are classified as math from text. The other 444 media posts were classified non-math, but math may well be in their images: the **17 Theorems of Entanglement** statements (Theorems 1–17, 2026-01-29/30, 2–3 images each) are visible only as titles. Screenshot math cannot be ruled out anywhere.
- **Classifier**: heuristic regex; F/N/non-math boundaries approximate (±5–10 % by spot check). "Distinct claim fragments" is a mechanical proxy, not a hand count of logical claims.
- **Grok-answered**: a floor; Grok-side capture was sampled (pre-rename @Brad75138648402 era especially). Strong/weak reply flags rest on API fields that were dropped on about half the pages.
- **Thread grouping**: 2,517 conversation_ids are session-inferred (see the `src` column).
- **Voice**: 94 math prompts look like pasted assistant output or third-person framing; whether those words are his own or another tool's is not verified.
- **Deleted/withheld parents**: 73 Not Found and 16 Authorization Error referenced posts (parent inventory §5). Prompts replying to them have no captured context.
- **Topic overlap**: one post often hits 3–6 topics; primary assignment depends on the priority order.

## 9. Prior art on disk (read-only, attributed; grades not adopted)
- `/workspace/quantum-comp/reports/FINDINGS_LOCAL_SOFTSF_COSMIC_JOURNEYS_X_AND_GROK_READONLY_2026-11-05.md` (sha256 0bca8e3206a960ffcf515e66b1808be624e0167aaad1ba2bb1026ecb45e401f4, sidecar OK): narrative Cosmic-Journey review; X search from 2026-09-15 only, ~20 linked posts.
- `/workspace/quantum-comp/reports/FINDINGS_LOCAL_SOFTSF_FILTERED_PROVABLE_MATH_X_GROK_COSMIC_JOURNEY_2026-11-05.md` (sha256 953be83a7e8031c41175fc4182ba89f0913b3755bfcf38d17449142759b024f9, sidecar OK): filtered list combining Grok chat exports with Bridge C / RTA X posts, carrying Soft SF tags (PROVABLE-CANDIDATE / NEEDS-DEFINITION / …). Its items (e.g. g_s(q) = Σ q^{n²}/(−q^s;q^s)_n, g_7 support mod 7, the 247 OccFilt_7 last-missing degree, M = [[2,7],[7,7]], ℚ(√35)) come mostly from **Grok exports**, not from X prompt text. Its tags are **not adopted** here.
- **What this catalog adds**: (1) full coverage of all 5,603 Category A prompts (2025-11-12 → 2026-11-10), not only the Sep–Nov Cosmic-Journey slice; (2) the Dec 2025 – Jun 2026 corpus (P1/P2: 1,572 math posts) is catalogued for the first time: master equations, the 539.9 s constant set, HQCC/ℛ₅₃₉.₉/Φ₁₁, 17 Theorems titles, Lagrangians, hadron/quark claims; (3) per-post Grok-answered and truncation flags; (4) recurrence counts and first/last dates per formula; (5) a finding that some figures discussed in Grok replies (4.12 ms, 242.7 Hz, 9760) do not appear in any visible prompt text.
